It can read as coordination, resumption, and circumstance, but its payoff is that revelation enters before the human plot has the last word, opening a new acoustic and agency strand. The particle also resolves the suspended temporal opening by moving from human claim and narrated deed into first-person divine voice.\",\"root_display\":\"— (no root)\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:وَ}} ({{tr:wa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"12:15:2","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d01fb8db637324756714","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"temporal conjunction setting the brothers completed actions as the trigger frame\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:لَمَّا}} ({{tr:lammā}}) sets the time-frame over the brothers completed going and resolving. The local grammar selects the temporal conjunction, not the negative homograph, so the point is a when-frame rather than denial. Its fixed adverbial form gathers human action and divine response into contact, the doubled mim audibly slows the hinge, and the expected answer to the time-clause is delayed until {{ar:وَأَوْحَيْنَا}} ({{tr:wa-awḥaynā}}) arrives.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ل م م}} ({{tr:l-m-m}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"grammaticalized temporal use from a root range of gathering/contact; local use is a fixed narrative timing operator, not the negative homograph\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:لَمَّا}} ({{tr:lammā}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"12:15:11","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d13f2dbacc90de3f50cb","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"definite concrete well/pit completing the hidden-recess construct\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:الْجُبِّ}} ({{tr:al-jubb}}) completes the construct and makes the hidden recess belong to a definite well already known from the plan in 12:10. The noun is rare in the Quran, appearing only in this surah, and has the force of a raw pit or cut-off place rather than an ordinary water-source scene. Its short, doubled closure gives the pit a closed sound, so the place meant to sever Yusuf from view becomes the exact anchor where proposal becomes deed.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ج ب ب}} ({{tr:j-b-b}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"cutting off and pit/well imagery; local noun selects the definite well, with severance and closed-depth pressure as image support\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:الْجُبِّ}} ({{tr:al-jubb}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"focus_occurrence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"12:15:17:1","source_type":"qac_morpheme","support_id":"sup_d1a32d099c08278770e7","text":"{\"lemma_ar\":\"يَشْعُرُ\",\"morph_features\":\"STEM|POS:V|IMPF|LEM:ya$oEuru|ROOT:$Er|3MP\",\"morpheme_role\":\"STEM\",\"pos\":\"V\",\"qac_ref\":\"12:15:17:1\",\"qac_word_ref\":\"12:15:17\",\"root_ar\":\"ش ع ر\",\"surface_ar\":\"يَشْعُرُ\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"12:15:20","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d3d07e328e42109c5340","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"circumstantial conjunction introducing the brothers non-perception state\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:وَ}} ({{tr:wa}}) opens the closing clause as both addition and circumstance: the brothers are in a state of not perceiving while the promised reversal is secured. The particle lets the closure say and and while at once, so their ignorance is not a loose afterthought but the condition around the promise. Its position after the emphatic disclosure makes the irony simultaneous and final, and the compact closing cadence turns concealment back on the plotters.\",\"root_display\":\"— (no root)\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:وَ}} ({{tr:wa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"12:15:13","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_d9e6b5ede5d21d88b812","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"Form IV first-person plural perfect of divine communication/revelation\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:أَوْحَيْنَا}} ({{tr:awḥaynā}}) reports completed divine communication in the first-person plural. The root quick and hidden communication image fits the well scene: while the brothers hide Yusuf from sight, a concealed divine channel reaches him and performs assurance rather than report only. This first divine communication to Yusuf outweighs the preceding human verbs, joins crisis-communication patterns such as 10:87 and 28:7, and anticipates the story later framing as unseen news revealed to the Prophet (12:102).\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:و ح ي}} ({{tr:w-ḥ-y}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"quick, hidden, directed communication;
